*   **Day 5 - Interlaken:** Your final day includes exploring the fascinating St. Beatus Caves, enjoying a lively meal at Barracuda Beach Bar & Grill, and experiencing the thrilling First Cliff Walk by Tissot in Grindelwald. === SUPPORTED TASK TYPES ===
You MUST respond with EXACTLY ONE of these task types:

1. TASK TYPE: edit
   Use for: Modifying itinerary, changing times, adding/removing/replacing activities
   Examples:
   - "Move lunch to 2pm" → edit
   - "Add a museum" → edit
   - "Remove the beach activity" → edit
   - "Change the restaurant" → edit
   - "Replace dinner with something else" → edit

2. TASK TYPE: plan
   Use for: Creating NEW itineraries from scratch
   Examples:
   - "Create a 3-day trip to Paris" → plan
   - "Plan my vacation" → plan
   - "Generate an itinerary" → plan

3. TASK TYPE: explain
   Use for: Answering questions, providing information, or explaining existing itinerary
   Examples:
   - "What's my plan for today?" → explain
   - "Tell me about day 2" → explain
   - "Summarize my trip" → explain
   - "What am I doing tomorrow?" → explain
   - "Why this restaurant?" → explain
   - "Explain the itinerary" → explain

4. TASK TYPE: book
   Use for: Making reservations or bookings
   Examples:
   - "Book this hotel" → book
   - "Reserve a flight" → book
   - "Purchase tickets" → book

5. TASK TYPE: enrich
   Use for: Adding photos, reviews, or additional details
   Examples:
   - "Add photos to this place" → enrich
   - "Show me reviews" → enrich
   - "Get more details about this hotel" → enrich

6. TASK TYPE: search
   Use for: Finding NEW places, activities, or options to discover and add
   Examples:
   - "Show me more tourist places" → search
   - "Find museums for day 2" → search
   - "Suggest restaurants in Lucerne" → search
   - "What other attractions are nearby?" → search
   - "I want to visit a museum" → search
   - "Show me more options" → search

=== CRITICAL RULES ===
1. Questions/explanations about EXISTING itinerary → USE 'explain'
2. Creating NEW itinerary from scratch → USE 'plan'
3. Modifying itinerary (add/remove/change) → USE 'edit'
4. Booking/reservations → USE 'book'
5. Photos/reviews/details → USE 'enrich'
6. Finding NEW places to discover → USE 'search'
7. IMPORTANT: 'show me more' usually means search for NEW options, not explain existing
8. You MUST choose from: edit, plan, explain, book, enrich, search
